# Test checklist - 2026-10

> Moved word for word from TEST-CHECKLIST.md on 2026-10-05: 2026-10-01 onward - new deploy sections go at the END of this file. Index of every section: [README.md](README.md).

## SkyyProfiles 0.1.5 - delete a profile (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. /profiles: a card that is not active shows Switch and a red Delete; the active card shows only "Active". The footer has a Close button.
2. Delete -> the footer row asks "Delete profile <name>? ... You have 6 hours to undo this." -> Cancel does nothing; Delete greys the card
   ("Deleted - restore it within 5 h 59 min") with a Restore button, and the slot is free at once ("N of 5 profile slots used" drops by one).
3. Restore -> the profile is back exactly (island, coins, skills, bags, items). Switch to it to check.
4. Limit: with every slot used, delete one, create a new one, then try Restore -> refused "No free slot ... Delete another profile first."
5. Commands: /profiles delete <name> (asks; the same command again within 10 s deletes), /profiles restore <name>, /profiles list marks
   DELETED with time left. Admin: /profileadmin archive list <player>, /profileadmin archive restore <player> <name>.
6. Server Setup -> Profiles -> "Undo window after deleting a profile" (hours, default 6). Set it to 1 to test the archive sooner: after the
   window the card is gone, /profileadmin archive list shows it, archive restore brings it back.
7. Note: the Delete / Switch labels are small - check they are readable (not cut with "...").
## SkyyClasses 0.1.10 - Warrior shield, Priest heal 100% (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. First start logs "no kit.Warrior / priestHeal.selfPercent line still had its 0.1.9 default - nothing changed (0.1.10 defaults marker added)".
2. Server Setup -> Classes: Warrior kit "Weapon_Sword_Crude:1,Weapon_Shield_Wood:1"; "Priest self-heal (% of what members get)" = 100.
3. New Warrior profile (empty off-hand): sword in the hotbar, Wood Shield equipped in the off-hand, right-click guards.
4. /classadmin kit <you> Warrior again while holding the shield -> the second shield goes to the hotbar. A torch in the off-hand stays put.
5. Priest in a party: your own heal equals each member's heal in the heal chat line.
6. Restart: nothing changes, no new History version.
## SkyyGuilds 0.1.5 - % disband refunds + contribution column (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. First start logs one GodSquad line "seeded once from its history ... COMPLETE"; a restart does not log it again.
2. /guild: wider window, new Contribution column - SkyLordPlayz 1,100, WesleyPlayz 0; Leader on top, then Admins, then Members, each group
   highest contribution first. A member who withdrew more than they put in shows a red negative number.
3. Deposit / withdraw: the number moves at once (deposit +, withdraw -).
4. Disband with two members who both put coins in: the confirm says "...back to the members by contribution (your share: X)"; after it, each
   purse got its share (60/40 contributions -> 60/40 of the bank). Nobody positive -> even split. An offline member's share lands in the
   profile they were last on.
5. Leaving (not the last member): the confirm says your contribution stays in the bank for the others. Last member leaving gets the whole bank.
6. /guildadmin info <guild> shows contributions and how the seed went.
## SkyyMenu 0.3.4 - menu item per profile, seconds in Server Setup (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. Join on your current profile: no second menu item arrives (/skymenu still replaces a lost one).
2. /profiles switch to another profile without the item: it arrives within ~6 s, once. A profile that already holds it gets nothing.
   A world switch, relog, or the item in a utility slot never gives a second copy. A new profile gets it within ~6 s.
3. /modconfig -> Skills, Advanced ON -> "Paid jump cooldown (s)" shows 0.8, help "0.2 to 600 seconds, default 0.8".
4. Type 0.19 -> refused ("must be 0.2 to 600 seconds"). Type 0.24 -> "0.24 s - saved" (file: acro.jumpCooldownMs=240). Then 0.25 -> 250.
5. Type 1,5 -> refused. Changes: "0.24 s -> 0.25 s", Undo asks in seconds and puts 0.24 back.
6. Classes, Advanced ON: "Class page open delay" Less/More step by 0.25 s.
7. Mods tile: Profiles 0.1.5 shows the delete/restore lines (archive lines for admins); SkyyUiProbe shows as a dev mod.
## SkyyGear 0.1.3 - world-chest gear unidentified, non-metal levels (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. First start logs "added 59 level family rows" once (a restart does not). Server Setup -> Gear -> Levels lists the new rows; Changes has
   59 undoable lines; History has "before the 0.1.3 level family rows".
2. Open a chest in a prebuilt structure (no loot table) with weapons/armor in it: they show unidentified straight away. Open it again
   (or another player does): nothing changes. Break an unopened one: the drops are unidentified.
3. Your own placed chests, island chests (starter chest), vault, bags, AH, trade: never touched.
4. SkyyExploration chest luck extra: a weapon/armor from it lands unidentified.
5. Stone Trork Daggers: Lv 5 (were 25). Bone sword 5, frost sword 25, Prisma armor 45, iron sword still 15.
## SkyySkills 0.4.11 - Priest heal XP 1 / 1.25 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. First start logs one "xp.properties updated for the 0.4.11 heal XP ... 0.2 -> 1, 0.25 -> 1.25, 300 -> 900" line (not again on restart).
2. /skills -> Divinity: "Healing pays 1 Divinity XP per HP on others, 1.25 on yourself (max 900 a minute)".
3. Heal a party member: Divinity XP = HP healed. Heal yourself: about 1.25 XP per HP. Wand kill XP is the same as before.
4. Server Setup -> Skills -> Combat: heal rows 1 / 1.25 / 900 ("Not multiplied by class skill XP"); Changes has 3 Undo lines.
## Accessories 0.5.2 + Sacks 0.7.10 - Workbench "Accessories & Bags" tab, Charcoal -> Smithing bag (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
1. Open a Workbench: a new tab at the right end (bag icon) named "Accessories & Bags". The Crafting tab now holds only the vanilla benches.
2. Order in the new tab: Accessory Bag, the five Normal bags, Normal accessories, bench accessories I, then Unique, Rare, Legendary
   (each: accessories, bench accessories, bags), Omni Accessory, Mythic Omni Bag LAST. If the game shows another order, say which -
   the client may sort on its own.
3. Pocket crafting still has the Accessory Bag. Craft one accessory and one bag from the new tab: same ingredients as before.
4. Charcoal: with a Smithing bag (or the Omni bag) in your inventory, charcoal you pick up into the MAIN inventory goes into the bag within
   ~2 s. Charcoal already in the hotbar stays there (all bag items work that way) - "Deposit all" on the Smithing tab takes it.
## SkyyIslands 0.5.5 + SkyyGuilds 0.1.6 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-01)
Islands (needs a second player, e.g. Wesley as co-op member of your island):
1. Delete the profile that OWNS the island (/profiles, Delete): Wesley can't /island visit or warp there ("...island is closed - its profile
   was deleted..."); if he is on it he is sent away within ~5 s. An admin can still go there.
2. Restore the profile: Wesley can go again, membership and settings unchanged.
3. (Optional, Server Setup undo window 1 h) let it archive: Wesley is released (told once, now or ~6 s after his next login) and can make
   his own island.
4. A member deleting their own profile never closes the island.
Guilds:
5. Deposit 1,000, then leave: the confirm says you get back 350 (35%) and the rest stays in the bank; your purse +350.
6. Kick a member with a positive contribution: the confirm says what they get back; an offline member gets it in their active profile.
7. Rejoin after leaving: your Contribution starts at 0. Server Setup -> Guilds -> "Leave refund" (0-100) changes the 35%.
8. Last member leaving still gets the whole bank; disband still splits by contribution.
## Phase 1 of the 2026-10-02 answers - Sacks 0.7.11, Collections 0.2.5, Essentials 0.1.7, UiProbe 0.3, Hud 0.3.11 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-02)
Bags (carry a Mining bag):
1. Take out 512 stone: it stays in your inventory. Mine 10: within 2 s exactly 10 go into the bag. Place 30, wait 2 s, mine 5: the 5 go in.
2. Deposit all on the Mining tab takes everything; relog after taking out 64 -> the 64 go back into the bag within 2 s.
3. Stack refill (Sacks page setting): Hotbar only - place 10 from a full hotbar stack -> back to full within 2 s, the bag shows 10 fewer.
   Full inventory - main-inventory stacks top up too (paused while a bench is open, tops up within ~2 s after closing). Off - nothing.
4. Carry 2 Normal Mining bags: the page shows "2 bags - up to ... of each item" ("their space adds up"). Drop one: nothing stored is lost.
5. Server Setup -> Bags and Crafting: "Stack refill from bags" off -> the page says the server turned it off; "Stack refill default".
6. NOT YET: benches / pocket crafting using bag materials (SkyySacks 0.7.12). /craft still crafts from your bags.
Collections:
7. A collection tier page no longer offers "Buy tier ... unlocks" for coins; tiers you already bought stay unlocked; bags still unlock.
Trade:
8. /trade: putting a Magic Bag or the Accessory Bag in the offer is refused; normal items still trade.
Vault arrow probes (admin, ~30 s each - report what you see):
9. /skyprobe win - does the game show a small page AND a chest window (with your inventory) at the same time?
10. /skyprobe secgrid - same, plus: can you drag items between the page's grid and the chest slots? (Each page says what to look for.)
HUD:
11. HUD editor -> add the "Skills" widget: Overall Level + your class skill by default; its Settings page toggles each skill line.
## Big round - SkyySkills 0.4.12 + SkyyGear 0.2 stage 1 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-02)
Skills:
1. Log: "class skills level on own table, max level 100 ... in-combat Mana regen 50%" and "class skill curve (first start of 0.4.12): 5 profile
   file(s) scanned, 0 with a higher class level now" (your profiles had little class XP, so nobody jumps).
2. /skills -> a class Stats page says "level N of 100"; your Archer / Priest / Warrior levels are unchanged.
3. Mage or Priest: spend Mana, get hit -> Mana refills about 2.5/s for 6 s after each hit (was 0), 5/s out of combat, nothing while
   charging a spell. /skills mana says IN COMBAT or out of combat with the rates.
4. Server Setup -> Skills -> Overall and Mana: "In-combat Mana regen" 0 = vanilla (no refill in combat), 100 = full; "Show my Mana Regen".
5. Server Setup -> Skills -> Levels: "Class level curve size" 150 shows the new totals; set it back to 100.
6. HUD Skills widget shows your class level; it updates within ~5 s after a live curve change.
Gear (damage and armor are still vanilla in this stage):
7. Log: one "updated to the 0.2 level bands" line. Server Setup -> Gear -> Levels shows Min | Cap (Copper 10 | 18, Armor_Copper 1 | 18).
8. A fresh Priest's kit Wood Wand: tooltip "Lv 1 - Requires Divinity 1" (green) and it hits.
9. Craft a Wood Wand (Workbench Survival tab or Weapon Bench Bow tab: 4 sticks + 6 fibre) -> made at your Divinity level (cap 13).
10. Craft a Copper Staff below Divinity 10 -> Lv 10, chat "Made at Lv 10 ... you need Divinity 10 (you: N)", red tooltip, hits do 0.
11. Queue 5 staffs below the band -> one chat line (throttled 10 s).
12. Copper Pickaxe below Mining 10 -> no chat line, tooltip "Lv 10 - Requires Mining 10 (coming later)" in grey, it still mines.
13. Iron / Mithril pickaxes keep their vanilla frame colour. A skill-80 crafter's Copper staff = Lv 18 ("Copper gear caps at Lv 18").
14. Change a band in Server Setup, then /gear relevel <player>: stamped items move into the new band.
15. Auction House: a Lv 4 and a Lv 9 wand show as different ("rolls differ").
## SkyyMobs 0.1 - mob levels (NEW mod, DEPLOYED 2026-10-02)
1. Server log: "[SkyyMobs] 0.1 ready", no WARN lines.
2. Look at a hostile mob (or the nearest within 8 blocks) and run /mobs platetest: it shows three test plates (three colour markups) - tell
   Claude which one shows RED in game (or none). Colours stay off until then; plates read "[Lv N] Name" in plain text.
3. Zone 1 mobs: levels 1-20 by biome (spawn plains low, rarer biomes higher, blue / Azure forest near 18-20). /mobs inspect on a mob shows
   its level, band and why (biome / environment / world).
4. Boars, Warthogs, Scaraks and Feran warriors get levels; cows, horses, deer, Kweebecs, traders and tamed animals never.
5. Deep lava (volcanic) caves: mobs use the zone's top band (Zone 1: 18-20).
6. Server Setup -> Mobs -> Difficulty: Easy / Normal / Hard / Custom - a higher level mob hits harder at once; health changes on the next
   spawn / chunk reload.
7. Higher-level mobs take longer to kill and pay more class skill XP (XP follows their max health).
8. Your own island and other island worlds: mobs have no level.
9. Restart: levelled mobs keep their level (saved on the mob).
## SkyySacks 0.7.12 - bags in bench + inventory crafting (DEPLOYED 2026-10-02)
1. Put sticks, rubble, logs and fabric scraps ONLY in your bags (the Omni bag is fine).
2. Workbench -> Survival -> Campfire: the bag amounts show and CRAFT is lit. If it still says 0/4: Server Setup -> Bags and Crafting ->
   "Bench chest count fallback" ON, reopen the bench, and tell Claude which case worked.
3. Craft once: /pd shows exactly 4 sticks and 2 rubble fewer. 5 quick crafts + Craft All: totals exact.
4. Cooking bench and Alchemy bench: craft with bag items.
5. Bench open: pick up sticks -> the count rises within ~1 s; drop the bag -> bag counts vanish; pick it up -> back.
6. Inventory crafting: the Accessory Bag from bag logs + scraps. If it still shows 0/4 -> tell Claude (pocket crafting fallback; /craft works).
   If opening the inventory ever misbehaves: Server Setup "Bags in inventory crafting" OFF (or bags.pocketCraft=false in config.properties).
7. A second player at the same bench does not see your bags. Profile switch with a bench open: counts vanish ~6 s, then the new profile's.
8. Close a bench mid timed craft: the started unit returns to your inventory, totals right. Hotbar refill keeps working with a bench open.
9. Server log: "craft link: benches and inventory crafting use the bags you carry (outbound filter true, pre-craft filter true, pocket window
   true)", "craft link: attached ..." lines, no "craft link failed", no "would go below zero".

## SkyyAccessories 0.5.3 - Night Vision accessory (DEPLOYED 2026-10-02, backup deploy-20261002-2335)
1. Log "[SkyyAccessories] 0.5.3 ready". As admin: /accessories givetier <you> NightVision Rare -> tooltip "Night Vision - see in the dark
   while in your Accessory Bag." with a Rare frame.
2. At night or in a dark cave, Equip it in /accessories: within about a second everything around you brightens; the bag page shows a
   "Night Vision" row.
3. A second player next to you sees no change. Unequip: the light goes out at once.
4. Equip again, switch worlds and relog: the light is back about a second after loading. Die and respawn wearing it: on, or back within 10 s.
5. Hold a torch at the same time: nothing breaks. A second Night Vision is refused ("already equipped").
6. Server Setup -> Accessories -> Night Vision: switch off -> light gone within a second; on again. Brightness: try 255,15,15,15 and
   15,15,15,15 first (the client probably uses 0-15 per colour); tell me which looks best. If you see a flicker every 10 s, set the re-send row to 0.
7. Switch to a profile without it: light off; switch back: on.
## SkyyMobs 0.1.2 - level health floor + live re-apply (DEPLOYED 2026-10-02, backup deploy-20261002-2355)
1. Log "[SkyyMobs] 0.1.2 ready ... health floor 50 HP".
2. /mobs inspect a Lv ~32 Snake_Cobra: "floor 50 HP applied (base 36 HP)", about 143 HP on Normal / 174 on Hard (was ~103 / ~125).
3. /mobs inspect a Skeleton_Scout: "base 61 HP (at or above the 50 HP floor)", same HP as before.
4. Hurt a mob to about half, then switch Difficulty Normal <-> Hard: its max health changes at once and it stays at about half (the log says
   "re-applied the health of N loaded levelled mobs"). WATCH THE HEALTH BAR: if it jumps (to ~36% or ~69%) the client missed the new max -
   walk away and back to fix it, and tell me (fix queued as 0.1.3).
5. Server Setup -> Mobs -> Strength -> Health floor 0: weak mobs drop to the old numbers at once; set it back to 50.
6. Kill a floored mob: kill XP is higher (it follows max health). Weak Lv 1 mobs (rats 21 HP) now have 50 HP too.

1. Log: "cooking.properties updated to the 0.1.4 Cooking XP default: xpMultiplier 1.0 -> 0.5" (only if you had not set 0.5 yourself),
   then "0.1.4 ready ... xpMultiplier 0.5".

4. A Grade 2 Meat Pie tooltip: "Grade 2 food - Cooking 20, or sooner with Cooking tree bonuses. Heal and buffs x1.64 stronger, buffs last
   x1.32 longer." - restores 24.6% health, +24.6% max health, duration 7:55. A Grade 2 Vegetable Skewer: heals 16.4% (was 13.2%).

6. Restart: no second update line; Server Setup -> Changes has the Undo line.
## SkyySkills 0.4.13 + SkyyHud 0.3.12 - combat widget, world-switch fix, Mana while charging (DEPLOYED 2026-10-03, backup deploy-20261003-0009)
1. Logs: "[SkyySkills] 0.4.13 ready ... + skill:fn:combat" and "[SkyyHud] 0.3.12 ready".
2. Get hit by a mob: within about 1 s a red "In combat 6s" box with a bar appears top centre and counts 6s ... 1s, then hides. IF YOU GET
   DISCONNECTED or see "Failed to parse or resolve document" at the first hit, tell me - rollback is SkyyHud 0.3.11.
3. Get hit again mid-countdown: back to 6s. Hitting a mob without taking damage does not start it.
4. /skyyhud: the editor shows the Combat Indicator; drag / resize it; Widgets -> Combat Indicator -> Out of combat: Show = a grey line.
5. Take a hit in the overworld and go to your island within ~2 s: the box keeps counting and ends ~6 s after the hit (not red for hours).
6. Out of combat with Mana below max, hold a charged wand / staff / bow attack: Mana keeps rising (5/s). After a hit while charging: 2.5/s.
   Admin /skills mana while holding says "charging - Mana still regenerates".
7. Profile switch right after a hit is refused for about 10 s (SkyyProfiles' combat lock), then allowed.
## SkyyBank 0.1.6 - stuck-page fix (DEPLOYED 2026-10-03, backup deploy-20261003-0028)
1. Open the SkyWynn Menu and click the Island tile; on the island type /bank. Within 1-2 s the page may redraw once with a blue line ("The
   game was holding back this page's clicks... fixed") and the log gets one "[SkyyBank] the game was dropping ... clicks" WARNING.
2. Click DEPOSIT ALL: a green "Deposited N coins" line and new numbers, no lasting "Loading...". (Clicked within the first second? you get
   the blue line instead - click again.)
3. WITHDRAW ALL, then 500 -> DEPOSIT, 2k -> WITHDRAW, REFRESH, Close: each answers.
4. Click DEPOSIT twice fast with 500 typed: each real click moves 500 once; purse + bank always add up to the same total.
5. KNOWN until the SkyyMenu fix: other pages (Vault, Menu, Sacks) can still ignore clicks after the menu's Island / Hub tile - /hub or
   /island clears it.

## SkyyWorldGen 0.1 - Zone 1 test island (NEW, DEPLOYED 2026-10-03, backup deploy-20261003-0209)
1. Log reaches "Hytale Server Booted" with no "Failed to validate" naming SkyWynn; "[SkyyWorldGen] ... ready ... template SkyWynn_Zone1 found".
2. Put valuable gear in a vault first (deaths should keep items now - not proven in game yet).
3. As op: /zone says Zone 1 "not created yet"; a normal player's /zone is refused.
4. /zone 1: "Creating Zone 1 - Emerald Wilds..." - you land on the south rim meadow facing the core; /zone info shows "Meadow rim".
5. Walk inward: meadow -> birch forest (~256 blocks out) -> azure core (~128). Vanilla mobs in each ring; SkyyMobs levels rim 1-3, middle 5-9,
   core 16-20.
6. With cheap items only, jump off the rim: you die below y -32, respawn at the landing and keep every item.
7. Server Setup -> World Gen -> Zone 1 landing point "0 1 0" is refused; /zone setlanding on the ground is accepted; set the default back.
8. /zone leave returns you to where you ran /zone 1; /hub from the island goes to the hub. Restart -> /zone 1 says "Teleporting..." (same island).
9. DON'T run /instances spawn SkyWynn_Zone1 (extra copies) or /sethub while standing on the island. The HUD shows the raw name skywynn_z1 (cosmetic).
## SkyySkills 0.4.14 - kill XP by level, roll XP, gathering boost, sickle XP (DEPLOYED 2026-10-03, backup deploy-20261003-0336)
1. Kill a levelled mob, e.g. a Lv 33 Skeleton Scout at Divinity 11: about 520 XP (was 108). An animal with no level pays as before.
2. Drop from a height that would cost a little health and CROUCH as you land: no damage and Acrobatics XP (~150-225); higher drops +50%.
   (If a crouch-landing gives nothing, tell me - the roll flag comes from the client.)
3. At Mining / Foraging / Farming 0-10: 3x XP; /skills stats shows the "Gathering XP x3 up to level 10..." line; it eases to x1.5 at 20.
4. Swing a sickle through ripe wheat: each ripe crop gives Farming XP (12 at Farming <= 10) and can double drop; unripe crops / potion plants
   give nothing from a swing. F on a ripe crop still pays exactly once.
5. Server Setup: new rows under Parts ("Kill XP by mob level"), Combat (six gap rows), Acrobatics ("Extra XP for a rolled landing"),
   Gathering (seven rows). Type decimals with a DOT (1.5) - a comma (1,5) is read as 15.
6. With tree bonus nodes, the Stats page line reads "Bonuses (trees + tools): ...".

## SkyyHud 0.3.13 - small boxes + combat colours (DEPLOYED 2026-10-03, backup deploy-20261003-0753)
1. Every HUD box hugs its text (turn Background ON for a widget to see the box); left / right gaps match the gap above / below.
2. /skyyhud: drag Game Clock into each corner (then Step + arrows to x 0 y 0) - it sits ~14 px from both edges at 170%.

## SkyyMerchants 0.1: roaming merchants (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 07:24, backup deploy-20261009-0724)
1. In-game steps were missing things.** I added a second restart, the 10-second confirm timeout, a no-coordinates rumour check and a hand-added Mythic row (steps 11, 23, 24 and 25 below).
5. **(Harness) The tests failed now and then.** Two of my first four runs failed in different places ("part ON: they come back", "zone2 back", "spawn refused"). The cause was in the mod: a spot search tried only 6 random columns. When the chosen player stands in the other zone, about half the columns fail, so a search failed roughly 1 time in 60. It now tries 16 columns (`SPOT_TRIES = 16`), and a search still stops at the first good spot. This also helps on the real server near zone borders. Check: `Core.SPOT_TRIES == 16`.
2. **PARTLY FIXED**
- **(Critic 2 #4, LOW) Empty tabs.** The tab-specific messages were already there, so the finding was half wrong. I made them clearer:
  - Mounts: "Mounts are coming soon - none for sale yet."
  - Pet eggs: "Pet eggs are coming soon - they arrive when pets come to the world."
3. **REJECTED**
4. - **(Critic 2 #2, MEDIUM-LOW) "Special weapons are mostly ordinary gear."** This is not a code fault. No real special weapons exist yet: the Armory specials are boss drops and are blocked, and our own Mythic specials haven't shipped. So the stock has to be placeholders. I've added it as question 4 for Skyy and left the craftable rows in.
5. - **(Critic 1) Hiding a row loses its stock.** Not a problem: a hidden row keeps its saved line, as the critic expected.
6. - **(Critic 2) The SkyyGear Mythic/Untiered/Set check does nothing yet.** Same as the first fixer said: it starts working on its own once SkyyGear reports those rarities. Until then the never-sell list is the only protection.
7. **Questions for Skyy:**
1. Are Fire Whip, Scorpion Flail and the Antique shields boss specials? My default says yes, never sold.
2. Do you want cali19's Traveling Mounts in the pack so merchants can sell mounts? My default is no until its licence is checked.
3. Are the prices OK? They are about 2–3k coins in Zone 1, 6–7.5k in Zone 2, 15–18k in Zone 3, 40k for Mithril in Zones 3–4 and 45k in Zone 4.
4. The stock is placeholder "specials" until our own special weapons ship. It is Armory recolours, vanilla rare and epic weapons, and craftable SkyyArmory wands, spellbooks, Bo staffs and a kunai. Is that OK for launch, or should merchants sell only non-craftable items?
8. **UNVERIFIED (needs the real game), unchanged:**
1. Whether the engine saves spawned NPCs with the chunk.
2. Whether F on the merchant opens the shop page.
3. Whether the nameplate and Interactable survive a restart.
4. Whether the surface environment ids read `Env_ZoneN_*` in world `default`.
5. Whether north is −Z.
6. The real NPC spawn, find and remove calls, chat to players and the world spawn point.
7. How the page looks on the client.
9. Also new and unverified: SkyyMerchants checks the SkyyArmory ids against `Mods\SkyyArmory.jar`. If SkyyArmory isn't loaded, those rows are skipped and nothing breaks.

3. With Chopping Speed (Foraging tree, first node) owned, chop logs. It should be just as fast as before this update.
4. With the same hatchet, hit a mob. It should swing at normal speed, not the fast wood speed.
5. Hold a pickaxe with the Mining speed node and mine stone. It should be just as fast as before.
6. If you have Tree Feller, chop a natural log. Extra logs on the same level should break, like before.
## SkyyGear 0.2.13: Crude weapon speed tiers + mace warnings (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 08:05, backup deploy-20261009-0805)
1. Join with the Crude Battleaxe (Rare, Earth / Water / Fire +1) in your inventory and hover it. It should now show "Attack Speed: ..." and a "Charged at ..." line, like the Copper one.
2. Hover your Crude sword, daggers or mace (class kit weapons). Each should show an Attack Speed line.
3. Swing a Crude weapon that says Fast or Super Fast. It should swing faster and each hit should do a bit less damage. A Slow one swings slower and hits harder. The swing sound may sound like the Copper weapon's; that is expected.

## SkyyFishing 0.1.2: real fishing line + idle bobber (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 08:36, backup deploy-20261009-0836)
1. Hold a rod without casting. You should see no line, and the red/white bobber hanging just under the rod tip, in first person and in F5. If it hangs sideways or upward, report it; that fix needs a rebuild.
2. Right-click open water about 6 blocks out, past lily pads, ferns or bank plants. It should cast, with no "Something is in the way".
3. Look at the line. It should be a thin off-white string from the rod tip to the bobber on the water, with no white glowing cloud. If no string shows at all, report it right away.
4. Check where the string starts, in first person and in F5. If it is off, open SkyWynn Menu > Server Setup > Fishing > Casting and bites and change "Line start: up" first, then "ahead" and "right" (a negative "right" moves it left).
5. While the line is out, turn your head quickly. The string should follow with no second, trailing copy.
6. Wait for a bite. You should see only a small splash at the bobber, and the string should follow the bobber as it dips.
7. Catch a fish by clicking fast, then keep clicking for a full 3 seconds after it lands, aimed at the bank. It should not cast or say "too shallow". Stop clicking, wait about 1 second, and click open water: it should cast.
8. Cast, then switch hotbar slots to put the rod away, switch back and right-click water at once. It should cast straight away, with no wait.
9. On a non-op account, repeat steps 2 and 3. Report whether the string is visible.
10. Aim at water 1 block deep: it should say "too shallow". Aim at water 2 or more blocks deep: it should cast.
11. With a second player nearby, check that they can see your string and where it starts.
12. After the session, check the server and client logs for any SkyyFishing warning, such as "fishing line could not be sent".

## SkyyPets 0.1: slot pets phase 1 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 13:48, backup deploy-20261009-1348)
1. Join the world. Chat should say you got your first pet, a Common Rabbit.

   - your pet list below.

5. Type `/petadmin give <yourname> Horse Legendary 100` and put it in the Pet slot. Your stamina bar should get bigger (+20) and you should move 5% faster; the Details page should say "+20 Max Stamina, +5 Speed %". Your max health should drop back down.
6. Open the Rabbit and press **Summon slot**. It should say the slot is locked and name the Stable quest.

8. Press **Take out** on that Rabbit. It should leave the Summon slot. The Horse stays in the Pet slot.
9. Type `/petadmin list <yourname>` to see the pet ids.
10. Open your first Rabbit (Common, level 1) and press **Pet slot**. Then type `/petadmin xp <yourname> Farming 5000`. Chat should say "Your Common Rabbit reached Lv ...".
11. Type `/petadmin take <yourname> <id>`, then `/petadmin restore <yourname> <id>`. The pet should come back with the same level. `/petadmin restore <yourname> -` should be refused with "is not a pet id".
12. Give yourself 8 more pets with `/petadmin give`, then open `/pets`. **Next** and **Prev** should page through the list.
13. In SkyWynn Menu > Server Setup > Pets, set "Summon slot unlock" to Closed. Pressing **Summon slot** should now say "closed on this server". Set it back to Stable quest.
14. Put the Legendary Rabbit back in the Summon slot. Change "Summon slot buff strength" in Server Setup, for example to 80. Without a restart, its Details should show the new percent and "+8 Farming Fortune".
15. If you use profiles, switch to another profile. It gets its own Rabbit. Switch back: your pets, levels and slots should all still be there, and no pet should get a sudden burst of XP.
16. If you have two classes, switch class with `/class`, wait 15 seconds, then open `/pets`. Pet XP should not jump.
17. Log out and back in. Everything should still be there.
18. Set Server Setup > Pets > Pets to OFF. Within a second the Horse's extra max stamina and 5% speed should go away. Turn it back ON.
19. On a non-admin account, try `/petadmin`. It should be refused. Note that `/petadmin` only works in game, not from the server console.
## SkyyWorldGen 0.3: archipelago proof world (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 13:48, backup deploy-20261009-1348)
1. Note your current game mode. Type `/zone`. There should be a line "/zone proof … not created yet".
2. Type `/zone proof`. The first time takes a few seconds while it builds. You land on the Arrival Isle at about (0, 151, 760), facing north.
3. Type `/zone info`. It should say Arrival Isle and "Landing point (fixed)".
4. Type `/tp 0 230 630`. You should see a solid land neck running north to the summit isle. Then `/tp 0 240 0`: blue Azure trees, with the top at about y 214.
5. Type `/tp -602 175 -219`. You should see the stone arch, with open sky below it and on both sides. Then `/tp 592 200 -216` for the land neck to Gorgeback.
6. Type `/tp 0 180 -620`. You should see the bridge gap: 40 blocks of void (z −640 to −601) with flat land on both edges.
7. Look down from any coast: there should be only empty void, no lava and no water anywhere.
8. Type `/zone info` in a few places. It should name the island you are on.
9. Type `/zone proof`, walk off the coast and fall. You should respawn at the landing with your items kept.
10. Before leaving, set your game mode back to the one from step 1 (for example `/gamemode adventure`); it is not reset on leaving. Then `/zone leave`, check your game mode in the hub, and check that `/zone 1` still takes you to the old island, unchanged.
11. To delete the proof world, and always before any rollback to 0.1: everyone types `/zone leave`, then stop the server. Delete `universe/worlds/skywynn_proof` and the `skywynn_proof.` lines in `mods/Skyy_SkyyWorldGen/worlds.properties`.

    - `/cast swap` out of combat swaps `/cast 1` and `/cast 2`. In combat it says "Leave combat first".

    - The rows "Most running abilities per world" (48), "Never hit (role name words)" and "Cooldown message" show.
    - Turn "Cooldown message" off and cast twice. The second cast only clicks, with no line. Turn it back on.
15. Be classless and type `/cast 1`: chat tells you to pick a class (or make a profile with one). As a Warrior it says "Warrior abilities are coming later…".
16. As a Priest with a wand, at full Health and nobody hurt nearby, type `/cast 1`. Chat says "Nobody within 9 blocks needs healing - nothing was spent." and no Mana is used.
17. As a Priest, get hurt, stand near a party member and type `/cast 1`. Both are healed (you more), a flash shows on each of you, and more healing ticks in over 4 s.

## SkyyGear 0.2.15: mining armor sets (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 16:03, backup deploy-20261009-1603)
1. Start the world and check the server log for the line "the 0.2.15 mining armor settings were added at the end". The 0.2.14 update line comes before it.
2. Open Server Setup → Gear → Gathering armor. You should see "Mining armor by tier" with 7 rows and the headings "Fortune Head Chest Legs Hands | Wisdom % Head Chest Legs Hands | Lamp 0-4". Under Gear → Rarity → Armor sets you should see the 7 Mining Sets.
3. Edit the Copper row: change the Chest Fortune from 0.2 to 1 (`0.1 1 0.1 0.1,0 0 0 0,1`). A Copper chestplate's tooltip should show "Mining Fortune +1" without a restart. Then type an invalid value such as `1 2 3` and check that it's refused. Put it back.
4. Craft a Copper chestplate. It should have a green Set label, say "Requires Mining N", and show "Mining Fortune +0.2" plus the grey "pickaxe or shovel in hand" line.
5. Wear 3 Copper pieces: the tooltip should say "Set: Copper Mining Set (3/4)" with the bonus in grey. Put on the 4th: it should turn green and say "Full set bonus: Mining Fortune +1". The tooltip stays green while you hold a sword. That's expected: the "hold a pickaxe" rule shows in `/gear`, not in the tooltip.
6. Hold a pickaxe and type `/gear`. The "Mining armor:" line should show Fortune +1.5 and say active. Hold a sword: it should say "hold a pickaxe or shovel".
7. Copper adds only about +1.5% extra-drop chance, so you won't feel it by mining. Use the `/gear` number, or for a visible test raise the Copper row's Fortune (for example `10 10 10 10`), mine stone, then put it back.
8. Craft a Cobalt piece while your Mining level is under 25. It should be made at Lv 25, show "Requires Mining 25 (you: N)" in red, and give no Health or mining lines.
9. Put on a metal piece above your Mining level: it should show the red "Requires Mining N (you: M)" line and give no Health. Check that a piece you can wear still adds Health.
10. Take an old metal piece made before today. It should keep its modifiers, say Mining, count for its set, and still reforge.
11. Try to Reforge a new green Mining Set piece. It should be refused with "fixed mining lines", and Level up should still work.
12. Open a Heavy mystery bag. It should give Bronze, Steel or Heavy Leather, never metal armor. If you have an old bag for Lv 38-44, it should now open (as the nearest level, for example Lv 37) instead of saying "No … exists".
13. Try to sell a crafted Copper piece on the Auction House. It's refused today; see Question 1.
14. Speed test: first turn Server Setup → Gear → General → "Level requirement check" off, because Adamantite needs Mining 35. Wear a full Adamantite set with a pickaxe in hand: you should walk a little faster. Switch to a sword: the extra speed should be gone within 1 s. Turn "Level requirement check" back on.
15. Mining Power: with "Level requirement check" off (Cobalt needs Mining 25), wear a full Cobalt set (+8%) and hit a block that takes many hits with a pickaxe. It may take one hit fewer; often you won't notice it.
1. 15b. Mining Wisdom: still with "Level requirement check" off, wear the full Cobalt set and hold a pickaxe. Each piece's tooltip should show "Mining Wisdom +x%", and `/gear` should show Wisdom +2% (+0.5, +0.7, +0.5, +0.3). Mine the same kind of ore with and without the set on and compare the Mining XP per block: it should be about 2% higher with the set. With a sword in hand it should go back to the normal amount. Turn "Level requirement check" back on.
16. Turn Server Setup → Gear → Gathering armor → "Mining armor (vanilla metal sets)" off. Metal armor should go back to 0.2.14 behaviour: class gate, no mining lines. Turn it back on.
17. Leave the world and rejoin. There should be no leftover speed, and the mining lines should come back.

## Ability R2+R3: HUD widget, Mana Barrier, Shield Bubble (DEPLOYED 2026-10-09 16:31, backup deploy-20261009-1631)
1. As a Mage, look under the Skills box on the left: there's an Abilities box showing the Meteor icon with "23 Mana", and Mana Barrier grey "Lv 10" below Sorcery 10.
2. Type `/cast 1`: the Meteor row counts down from 14s with a shrinking bar, then shows "23 Mana" again.
3. Spend Mana below 23: the Meteor row turns grey.
4. Hold crouch: within about 2 s the rows change to your alts (Frost Nova, Starfall). Let go and they change back.
5. Open `/skyyhud` and move, hide or show the box. If the icons look broken, go to its Settings, set Icons to Hide, and tell me.

52. ## Partly rejected
1. **"Server refuses to start without 0.5" (engine critic, MEDIUM): the shutdown claim is wrong; the rollback-floor request stands.**
   - **Why the claim is wrong:** in the server jar, `Universe.start` only shuts the server down for a world that fails to load when it is started with the `--verify-worlds` option. On a normal start it calls `loadWorldFromStart(..).exceptionally(...)`, which logs one SEVERE "Failed to load world: %s" and keeps running.
   - **What I changed:** the build header's rollback note now says this, including the `--verify-worlds` exception.
   - **Left for the main session:** a ROLLBACK FLOOR comment in `tools\deploy_set.py` is still worth adding, since I may not edit that file. Suggested text: "don't roll back, remove or disable SkyyWorldGen below 0.5 while `universe/worlds/skywynn_proof2` exists; stop the server, delete that folder and its `skywynn_proof2.*` lines in `worlds.properties` first."

6. Switch to Creative or fly first, because the world is Adventure mode. Then dig into the bottom layer (below y 40) on each of the 5 islands: the summit, south, west, east and north. The stone skin hides lava from below, so you have to dig in. Check for:
   - lava caverns and ores
   - cave decoration and water pockets
   - cave mobs spawning
7. Visit the joins from the summit island:
   - Southern island: land neck.
   - Western island: thin rock arch.
   - Eastern island: land neck.
   - Northern island: a 40-block gap for a future bridge.
8. Open the map. It should show islands, not land in the void. BetterMap only picks up the new world after the deploy.
9. Stop and restart the server, then `/zone proof` again. You should land in the same world.
10. Type `/zone leave` to go back. Report any lag while flying, missing mobs, or odd edges.

66. ### gear0218
67. - Nothing can be lost or duplicated; the feature just doesn't work in normal play. The Multitools mod is `Enabled: False` in Skyy's world right now, so deploying is harmless.
- Ways forward (a main-session decision, probably a new round):
  - **A. Keep it at the bench.** SkyyGear adds a resource type per tier to the vanilla pickaxes, hatchets and shovels at runtime, plus matching resource type definitions. The recipes then take that resource type, which ignores item data, like vanilla "any Wood" recipes. But a rolled tool could then be used up, because the bench takes the first match in slot order, so it needs a craft guard. Whether the game client shows such a recipe as craftable is unverified.
  - **B. Our own assembly.** A Gear command or page button takes 1 pickaxe + 1 hatchet (+ shovel) of a tier, preferring plain or lowest-value ones and refusing a rolled one unless confirmed, then gives the multitool. The Multitools mod's bench recipe would be removed. This can be fully tested in the harness, but it is not "crafted at a bench".
  - I'd recommend A if Skyy wants the bench, otherwise B.

## Soul Orb: damage, vanilla essences, faster tether, clearer lines, Wings of Fate (DEPLOYED 2026-10-11 00:22, backup deploy-20261011-0022)
1. Craft a Soul Orb at the Weapon Bench from 25 Life Essence, 4 Sticks and 1 Plant Fibre. No Blue Crystal should be needed.
2. Check the cage recipes: Thorium uses Ice Essence, Cobalt uses Water Essence, and Onyxium uses 1 Concentrated Life.
3. Check that Thorium is ice-cyan, Cobalt water-blue and Onyxium lime, and that the cage tooltips show damage per second, the tether time and a Wings of Fate line.
4. Hold right click on one mob with a Thorium Cage. Read the number on the hit: about 7.5 per tick before gear levels and defence, not 2.
5. While a Binding is forming, its line should be thin, faint and flickering. After about 1.35 s it should turn bright and thick.
6. If you have a high-tier cage, bind many mobs. Tethered lines should be clearly denser and brighter than untethered ones.
7. Kill Zone 2 mobs (Water Essence can drop) and Zone 4 mobs (Lightning). Earth and Light Essence should never drop, and their tooltip should say they're no longer used.
8. Hold left click for about half a second and let go while looking at open ground. You glide in 3 bounds with blue wings and a trail, using 20 Mana and 10 Stamina.
9. This one needs a second player or an alt. Bind some mobs, then hold left click aimed at them within 30 blocks. You're carried to them, they heal, and you both get a chat line. Your stored healing is then empty.
10. Do step 8 while falling from high up. You should still take fall damage.
11. Do step 8 facing a wall. If you don't move, the 20 Mana and 10 Stamina come back.
12. Check that Server Setup > Armory > Soul Orb + essences shows the 16 `wings.*` rows and the damage-per-second table.
## Requip (wardrobe v2) + /smithing hub + Void hotbar refill (DEPLOYED 2026-10-11 01:38, backup deploy-20261011-0138)
1. ### Requip (SkyyWardrobe 0.2)
2. **Before you start:** put an iron sword, a pickaxe, an iron helmet, a stack of health potions and a stack of bread in your storage (not the hotbar). Put some stone in your Magic Bags (`/pd`).
1. Join and wait 2-3 s. A chat line says your old Wardrobe moved into Requip.
2. Type `/requip` (or `/rq` or `/wardrobe`).
3. If the page asks "Use what you wear now for it?", click **Yes** or **No** first. Only after that do the **Returned items** and **Take one out** buttons appear, if you have any.
4. Click **Your inventory**. Page 1 is your hotbar plus storage. Click the sword: it goes into Requip. Click a stone block: it refuses and says why. Click **>** for page 2 (worn armor plus backpack), then **<** to go back.
5. Click the potion stack and the bread stack. They land in the Items tab with their counts.
6. Click **Deposit all gear**. The pickaxe and helmet go in, and your hotbar stays.
7. Click Loadout 2 in the left list. Click its Head slot, then the helmet. Click Hotbar 1, then the sword. Click Hotbar 2, then the potion stack. Click **Equip**.
8. Click Loadout 3. Click Hotbar 3 and pick the same sword, then Hotbar 1 and pick the pickaxe. Click **Equip**. The sword moves from slot 1 to slot 3, and there is still only one.
9. Still on Loadout 3:
   - Click Hotbar 4, make sure nothing is held in that hotbar slot, then click **Pocket**. A "Blocks in your Magic Bags" list appears on the right. Click the stone. Hotbar 4 now shows a "P" pick.
   - Click Hotbar 5, then **Empty**. Click Head, then **Keep**.
10. Click **Rename**: type a name in the box at the bottom, then press Rename. Then click **Equip**. Hotbar 4 fills with stone from your bags.
11. Wear a full set, click Loadout 4, then click **Use what I wear**.
12. Pets and abilities:
    - Set a pet with SkyyPets and pick your abilities in the class menu.
    - Back on the Requip page, click **Save pets/abilities**.
    - Equip another loadout, then Equip this one again. The pet and abilities come back.
13. In the Armor tab, click the **Sort** button a few times (newest, then rarity, then level). Type "iron head" in the search box and press **Search**: iron helmets show. Press **Clear**.
14. Click an item that no loadout uses, then **Take out**. It comes back as a normal item.
15. Click the sword (it is picked in loadouts 2 and 3) and press **Take out**. A warning appears. Press **Take out** again to really take it out.
16. Click Loadout 2 and press **Reset**. A warning appears; press **Reset** again. Every slot goes back to Keep.
17. Scroll the loadout list. Loadouts past your level show "- locked".
18. Auto-store:
    - Click the **Auto-store** button so it shows ON. Your own hotbar tools stay.
    - Hold slot 1, then pick up a sword from the ground (it lands in another hotbar slot). Within about 2 s it goes into Requip, and what you hold stays.
    - Take that sword out again (press twice if a loadout uses it). It stays with you.
    - Turn auto-store OFF.
19. Open SkyWynn Menu > Server Setup > Requip. Check the rows, including "Auto-store takes hotbar pickups", and change one value.
20. If the page shows **Returned items**, click **Take one out**.
21. Try `/requip 2`, `/requip dump`, `/requip auto on` and `/requip auto off`.
22. Reforge a picked item while it is on you, Equip another loadout, then Equip back. It is still the same single item.
23. Drop a picked item and open Requip. It says "NOT WITH YOU". Click it, then **Forget** (press twice). Pick it up again and deposit it as a new item.
24. Die with a picked item, pick it back up and open Requip. Tell me whether it says "on you" or "NOT WITH YOU".
25. **Needs SkyySacks 0.7.17 (R2) deployed first:** Equip Loadout 3 (with the pocket stone), then Equip a loadout without it. The stone goes back to your bags.
26. Send screenshots of the page. I'll check the server and client logs afterwards.
3. ### /smithing (SkyyGear 0.2.23)
4. Before testing: the Craft buttons need SkyySacks 0.7.17 deployed (the live set has 0.7.16). The Requip steps (6, 10, 11b, 12) need SkyyWardrobe 0.2. Without them you'll see "No SkyySacks" or "Requip is not loaded" instead.
1. Type `/smithing` (or `/smith`). The page is titled Smithing, with tabs Craft | Reforge | Upgrade | Multitool and Craft selected.
2. With Requip not loaded, the footer should read "your inventory (Requip is not loaded)".
3. Click each category on the left:
   - The page opens on Gathering armor.
   - Combat armor shows the "can be crafted on this server yet" message (question 1).
4. On Weapons, click "Show all", then "My class". A Warrior sees only swords, longswords and spears.
5. Open a Gathering armor set card. Check:
   - the body-shaped layout, the level, "Mining", Health and Resist, the set bonus and "x/4 craftable";
   - materials in white (enough) or gold (short);
   - a greyed button names its reason, with a gold line above the rows giving the full reason.
6. With the Armor Bench accessory equipped and materials in your inventory or bags, click Craft. The info bar should say "Made: … -> Requip", and the piece should appear in Requip without ever showing up in your inventory.
7. Take the accessory off. The buttons should read "Locked", and the gold line names the accessory.
8. Put it back and click "Craft every missing piece". Only pieces you don't already have are made.
9. Switch "Crafted gear goes to" to Inventory, craft once, and check the item lands in your inventory.
10. Click the Reforge tab, then Craft again. Gear stored in Requip shows under "In Requip". Click one and it goes onto the anvil; "Return to Requip" puts it back.
11. On Upgrade:
    - (a) Keep fewer bars in your inventory than needed and the rest in your bags, then upgrade a Soul Cage. It should use the bag bars.
    - (b) Upgrade a Soul Cage or tool that is stored in Requip (fetch it from the list first). It should still be in its Requip loadouts afterwards.
12. In Multitool, merge a pickaxe and a hatchet that are both in Requip. The multitool should still be in your Requip loadouts.
13. Re-identify a mystery-bag item that is stored in Requip (fetch it first). It should still be in its Requip loadouts afterwards.
14. In SkyWynn Menu → Server Setup → Gear → Smithing, check both rows are there ("Smithing Craft tab" and "Class armor types"), then turn "Smithing Craft tab" off:
    - the Craft tab disappears from the tab row;
    - `/smithing` shows a gold "switched off" line and opens Reforge instead.
    - Turn it back on.
15. Click quickly between categories, and press Refresh several times. The page should never lag or end up in a wrong state; an extra click may just do nothing.
16. Afterwards, send both logs (server and client).
5. ### SkyySacks 0.7.17 (hotbar block refill, craft bridges, /craft class filter)
6. In Requip pick a hotbar slot as a Pocket block (e.g. Stone) and Equip: it fills from your bags and refills within ~2 s as you build; another item in that slot is never touched.

Equip Floor and Duration+ together on Blood Frenzy, and Seeker and Ricochet together on Rapid Fire (do these once you are on each class). Each pair should be refused.

4. **Rallying Guard:** You should take less damage, and nearby mobs should turn on you. With Echo, the damage cut should last about 3 s longer. A party member 10 blocks away should be covered; a non-party player 8 blocks away should not.
5. **Shield Shockwave with the shield in the off-hand:** use it on a group. They should be hit and stunned. With Echo, a weaker cone follows 1 s later.
6. **Shield Shockwave with no shield:** take the shield out of the off-hand and press it again. It should be refused with "needs a shield in your hand or off-hand - nothing was spent", and your Mana should not move.
7. **Iron Chain:** use `/cast swap` (or the panel) to put it in. Use it on a mob 10 blocks away; the mob should be yanked to you.
8. **Challenge and Guardian's Oath:** Type `/classadmin abil <you> pickA` and use Challenge on a mob. You should take less damage from it, and some should reflect. Type `pickB` and use Guardian's Oath on a party member. Part of their damage should move to you.
9. **Shields for other classes:** have a non-Warrior friend hold a shield. Their weapon hits should deal no damage.
10. **Switch to Berserker:** Type `/profileadmin setclass <you> <profile n> Berserker`. Type `/classadmin kit <you> Berserker` (the kit does not drop automatically after an admin class change). Run grant and maxlevels again, then buy A1 to A4 in `/tree`.
11. **Enrage:** Have a player 6 blocks away and a party member 12 blocks away, then use Enrage. Then have them walk 30 or more blocks away. Their buff should stay until it ends, because targets are picked once and it is not an aura. Your hits should grow stronger.
12. **Whirlwind and Earthsplitter:** Use Whirlwind. It should spin and heal you a little; with Echo, a weaker spin follows. Swap and use Earthsplitter. It should send a line shockwave.
13. **Blood Frenzy:** Turn it on and swing at the air. Mana and Stamina should drop on every swing. Hit mobs; the stacks should climb. Have an ally step out of the aura (over 8 blocks for a player, 16 for party) and back in. Their buff should go and come back. Swing a pickaxe at a block. It should cost nothing. With Floor equipped, reach 5 stacks, stop hitting for 10 s, and check that the stacks stay at 5 (10 at Lv 2). Press it again to turn it off.
14. **Blood Frenzy and changing world:** turn it on, then type `/hub`. You should see "Blood Frenzy off - you changed world", and the next press should turn it ON.
15. **Warlord's Banner:** Plant it. You should get +16 %, your party +12 % and other players +8 %, for both damage and defence (getting hit should hurt less). Kill mobs inside it; its time should grow. Leave its range. It should fall, and the 40 s cooldown should start at the fall. Plant it again, then `/hub`. You should see "banner fell - you changed world".
16. **Banner and quitting:** plant it, quit, and rejoin within 40 s. The Banner should be ready, not on cooldown.
17. **Switch to Archer:** Type `/profileadmin setclass <you> <n> Archer`, then `/classadmin kit <you> Archer`. Run grant, maxlevels, buy A1 to A4, and pickA or pickB as needed. Equip Seeker on Rapid Fire and Echo on Explosive Arrow and Arrow Rain in the panel.
18. **Archer abilities:** Pinning Shot: the mob should be rooted and marked. Rapid Fire with Seeker: arrows passing near enemies should hit them. Explosive Arrow: use it, then shoot a bow at a Marked mob in a group. It should blast; the neighbours should take no more than the plain blast. With Echo, a second blast follows 1 s later. Arrow Rain with Echo: a second shower should follow. Hunter's Net: mobs inside should be rooted.
19. **Server Setup:** open SkyWynn Menu, then Server Setup, then "Warrior Bers. Archer". Change one number and recast; the change should show.
20. **Logs:** send both the server and client logs afterwards. `/classadmin abil <you> info` shows "frenzy swings paid".
## REBIRTH STATS + SKILLS SOUNDS: SkyySkills 0.4.39 + SkyyRebirth 0.3.3 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-14 19:30, backup deploy-20261014-1930)
1. Mine until a skill levels up. You should hear the "upgrade" sound with the SKILL LEVEL UP line. If your Overall Level also goes up, you hear only the Overall sound.
2. Roll just before a monster hits you. You should hear a whoosh with "Perfect dodge!".
3. In /settings, hide "Skill level-ups", then level up again: no line and no sound. Turn it back on.
4. This needs SkyyMenu 0.3.25 deployed. In /settings turn Sounds off, then level up a skill and do a perfect dodge: both should be silent. Turn Sounds back on.
5. As admin (rebirth can stay OFF): Note your max Health. Run `/rebirthadmin points class 100`, then /rebirth → Class arm → buy Vitality levels. Within about 1 s, max Health goes up 1 % per level, and the node panel says "now (SkyySkills: max Health)". To test Focus, play as a Mage or read the Stats page. The gain is small for a non-Mage (5 levels add about +0.5 Mana).
6. Buy Class Wisdom levels, then earn class XP. You should get about +2 % XP per level. Gathering skills such as Mining should not change.
7. Per-rebirth boost: test this on a throwaway profile, because a rebirth resets your skills, coins and bank. Run `/rebirthadmin dryrun` first. Then type `/rebirthadmin rebirth` twice within 10 s. This works with rebirth OFF. Max Health, Mana and Stamina should each rise 3 %. The Stamina change is small, so check the Stats page.
8. With SkyyProfiles, switch profile while you have Vitality levels. The extra Health should go away within about 1-2 s and not come back on a profile without nodes. Switch back and it should return.
9. Run `/rebirthadmin refund class`. The extra Health and the class XP bonus should be gone within about 1 s.
10. Go to SkyWynn Menu → Server Setup → Skills → General and switch "Rebirth stat boosts" off. Every rebirth bonus should disappear. Turn it back on.
11. Go to Server Setup → Skills → Sounds: Switch "Skill level up sound" off: no sound. Type `SFX_Nope` as an id: it should be refused with a message. Enter any id from /soundtest: that sound should play instead.
## SETTINGS SEARCH + MOD SOUNDS SWITCH + SHARDS ADMIN BUTTONS: SkyyMenu 0.3.25 (DEPLOYED 2026-10-14 19:30, backup deploy-20261014-1930)
